consequences

Status
Accepted
Context
Meronq
integrates
with
many
external
systems
Git

Notion
build
tools
compilers
test
runners
IDEs
Unity
Docker

data

If

Decision
Meronq

Model

Meronq
concepts
first
Pipeline
Core

Entity
Relation
Evidence
Event
Action
Result

may

Result

may

Repository
State

the

Model

Meronq
concepts
Consequences

id
link
stability
Status
Accepted
Context
Engineering
knowledge
is

static
Some

is

and
frequently
used
some

old
but

important
some
is

as
evidence
Before

decay

how

That

This

decides
only

The

it
is

in
ADR
Decision
Meronq

knowledge
usage
as

only

the
knowledge
itself
Two

Reinforcement

Recorded

used
or

validation
Table
Access

or

Facts
entities
relations

or

usage
Usage
is

signal
Principle
Meronq
does
not

facts
Usage
is
recorded
never
destructive
to
facts
Implementation
status
Implemented
in
schema
v
surfaces
event
counts
visible
in
Handshake
output
Usage

is
durable
across

upserts
CEM
rows
and
archives
the
ones
a
snapshot
omits

them
Sprint
schema
v
is
aggregated
on
read
via
which
separates

usage
from
bookkeeping
sync
reinforcement
and
bulk

access
sweeps
mean
presence
not
use
hits
reinforce
the
entities
they
return
Sprint
Entity

ranks
by
recency
weighted
strength
instead

alphabetically
and
Handshake
orders

CEM

way
Sprint

none
outstanding
The

model
consuming
this

Meronq
Principles
These
principles
define
how
Meronq
is
designed
implemented
and
evolved
Every
architectural
decision
should
reinforce
these
principles
LLMs
understand
language
Meronq
understands
engineering
Large
Language
Models
are
strong
at
reasoning
over
language
Meronq
is
responsible
for
understanding
engineering
systems
and
translating
them
into
a
shared
engineering
model
LLMs
should
reason
not
parse

build
logs
compiler
output
Git
history
repository
structure
and
tool
output
is
Meronq
s
responsibility
AI
clients
should
spend
their
context
window
on
reasoning
not
on
understanding
raw
engineering
data
Project
understanding
should
be
idempotent
across
AI
clients
Different
AI
models
may
reason
differently
They
should
not
understand
the
project
differently
Meronq
provides
a
shared
engineering
model
that
allows
different
AI
clients
to
operate
from
the
same
understanding

evidence

Meronq
